- **shiploop-complete**: Run exactly the callback the current packet printed on its line labelled `Callback for this stage` (an Improve stage's packet labels it `Next command (` or `2. - **shiploop-next**: Reprint the current packet without advancing the run: ([whichguy/skill-craft](https://github.com/whichguy/skill-craft/blob/HEAD/plugins/skill-craft/skills/shiploop/commands/shiploop-next.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **shiploop-recover**: ~~~sh python3 "$SKILL_ROOT/scripts/shiploop" pause --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --reason='specific blocker' python3 "$SKILL_ROOT/scripts/shiploop" resume --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" python3 "$SKILL_ROOT/scripts/shiploop" halt --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --reason='terminal reason' python3 "$SKILL_ROOT/scripts/shiploop" delegation --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --set inline # or ask-agent ~~~ ([whichguy/skill-craft](https://github.com/whichguy/skill-craft/blob/HEAD/plugins/skill-craft/skills/shiploop/commands/shiploop-recover.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **shipmates-auto-trunk**: Shipmates: Run a test-driven, trunk-based development loop — parse a goal into a task list, write a failing test first, implement the smallest change, run the repo's checks, and commit to trunk. Repeat until the task list is empty and checks are green. Commits and pushes are opt-in. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-auto-trunk.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-consolidate-issues**: Shipmates: Review every open issue against git history, close what's already done or stale, migrate the legacy ones, slim the backlog, and bundle the survivors into themed groups so similar work ships together efficiently. Read-only by default — it reports; changes happen only on an explicit apply. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-consolidate-issues.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-deslop**: Shipmates: Audit a codebase for health debt — dead code, duplication, redundancy, over-engineering beyond actual need, inconsistency, bad patterns, misplaced files and folders, dependency and config rot — grade every finding by risk, then file the work as an epic with sub-issues. Never edits the codebase: it analyses, files tracked work, and stops at a human gate where the captain chooses what ships now and what waits. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-deslop.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-document**: Shipmates: Write or refresh documentation that actually works — the technical-writer drafts it from the real code, then a fresh reader agent follows the steps against the repo and must reach the stated result. Loops until the docs are drift-free and completable. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-document.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-fix-bug**: Shipmates: Fix a bug the honest way — reproduce it as a failing test first, root-cause it, apply the minimal fix, and prove it with the test flipping red→green while the suite stays green. Worktree-isolated, CI-gated, opens a PR. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-fix-bug.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-harden**: Shipmates: Security-harden a surface — threat-model it, rank the findings, and re-review until every finding has a fix or an explicit accepted-risk note (and secrets/dependency scans are clean). Read-only by default — it reports; remediation happens on a branch, opt-in. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-harden.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-migrate**: Shipmates: Run a mechanical migration across a whole codebase — discover every call site, transform each in isolation, verify per-site, and gate on a clean sweep (no old-pattern remnants) with the suite green. For API/dependency/pattern/framework migrations. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-migrate.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-onboard**: Shipmates: Read an unfamiliar repo and write the agent-facing context file every other command depends on — conventions, commands, boundaries and the quality bar, proven by running them. Gated on a fresh agent answering the crew's real questions from the file alone. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-onboard.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-plan-epics**: Shipmates: Turn a brief (or several) into a tracked backlog — GitHub epics + linked, labelled user stories, with a context-selected planning panel authoring and reviewing in parallel. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-plan-epics.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-polish**: Shipmates: Iterate a visual / UI / output artifact to a specialist's sign-off — produce → critique → fix, looping until the art-director, ux-ui-designer, or product-manager is genuinely happy (or a round cap). ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-polish.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-pr-review**: Shipmates: Run the specialist acceptance board against an existing pull request the crew didn't author — classify the diff, pull the right reviewers, and return one consolidated verdict. Read-only by default: it reports, it never repairs. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-pr-review.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-qa**: Shipmates: Walk a captain through interactive, one-step-at-a-time local QA of a PR, issue branch, or named branch — risk-targeted by default, optional blind smoke — logging findings for handoff. Guides and reports; it never repairs. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-qa.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-refactor**: Shipmates: Change the shape of the code without changing what it does — pin current behaviour in characterization tests first, transform, then prove equivalence by those tests passing unmodified and no existing test being deleted, skipped or loosened. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-refactor.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-release**: Shipmates: Cut a release safely — assemble the changelog from what actually merged, bump the version, gate on green CI at the tag, run an SRE pre-flight (rollback + migration safety), then tag and (opt-in) publish. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-release.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-report-bug**: Shipmates: File a structured bug report on the Shipmates repository from a live run — harness, command, repro, expected vs observed, in the format maintainers triage. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-report-bug.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-ship-epic**: Shipmates: Ship an epic by driving /shipmates-ship-issue over its stories in dependency-ordered waves — independent units fan out concurrently; one epic plan amortizes overhead, gate stories pause for sign-off. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-ship-epic.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-ship-issue**: Shipmates: Take one or more GitHub issues/stories from open → reviewed PR (→ merged, opt-in) autonomously — worktree, subagent build, CI gate, specialist acceptance board, absorb-first nit disposition. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-ship-issue.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-spike**: Shipmates: De-risk an open technical decision with a time-boxed spike — frame the question, prototype several approaches in parallel as throwaways, judge them against the project's constraints, and return a recommendation as an ADR. Produces a decision, not production code. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-spike.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-upgrade**: Shipmates: Check for a newer Shipmates release, then refresh, audit, and repair every known install — reporting only when asked, filing upstream bugs only when asked. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-upgrade.md) · ★6)
